2026-10-23T04:45:09.2393176Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] Serve the FA3 verify candidates and the repaired dense GEMM selection on image 39899235870

Pin image 39899235870 (build of 89936a3):
- sha256 3b3d4a8d46671f6264204b35ea797e3f34aad91e6ec806ae252ece77ec22f8c8
- 12270067712 bytes
It adds the companion /opt/apac/fa3-verify library next to the unchanged sgl_kernel flash_ops. Every other serving setting is main 3642c64: hot-4 Prefill replication, cap 128, concurrency 192 and EAGLE3 steps 3 / draft 4.

The FA3 verify selector runs on the head node's spare four GPUs during the numeric gate, with a 175-second limit. It compares the current TARGET_VERIFY FA3 (N96, non-TMA, with the B4 metadata sharing) against the candidates on the same KV inputs and complete 94-layer graphs, in two alternating blocks:
- t64s2 / t64s3: N64 with paged TMA, 2 or 3 stages;
- p128s2: N128 non-TMA;
- t64split2 / t64split3: N64 TMA with a fixed split and a dedicated FP32 combine.
Only b32 decides, and a candidate needs at least 3.2 us per layer on every GPU and in both blocks. b33 to b36 are recorded for the cap decision.

The dense selector runs on the Decode GPUs while the Prefill workers start. It is retried without the FlashInfer candidate, whose TensorRT-LLM JIT cache stopped the whole selection in 39881882325. A failing candidate is now excluded and recorded instead of aborting the others.

Candidates that do not win, and selectors that fail, leave the Decode worker on the current kernels. There is no [profile] tag because the 17-minute limit has no room for the profile pass next to the selection. Results go to results/decode-selection/.
